        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a 14-step plan across 5 phases touching frontend contexts, gallery interactions (click/lasso/drag), backend payload extension, and agent chat wiring. Key execution risks: (1) The GallerySelectionProvider must mount in AppProviders.tsx high enough for both GenerationsPane (layout-level) and AgentChat (VideoEditorShell-level) to consume it. (2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline. (5) Multi-item drag requires a new drag type that the timeline drop handler must explicitly check before the existing single-item path.\n\nExecution order: T1-T3 are foundation (no UI changes, safe). T4-T6 add click/lasso/visual feedback. T7-T9 handle multi-drag. T10-T12 are backend (can parallel with T7-T9). T13-T14 wire everything together. T15 validates.\n\nThe SelectedMediaClip type currently has { clipId, assetKey, url, mediaType }. Gallery items won't have clipId or assetKey \u2014 the executor should use empty strings or generate synthetic IDs. - [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a 14-step plan across 5 phases touching frontend contexts, gallery interactions (click/lasso/drag), backend payload extension, and agent chat wiring. Key execution risks: (1) The GallerySelectionProvider must mount in AppProviders.tsx high enough for both GenerationsPane (layout-level) and AgentChat (VideoEditorShell-level) to consume it. (2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline. (5) Multi-item drag requires a new drag type that the timeline drop handler must explicitly check before the existing single-item path.\n\nExecution order: T1-T3 are foundation (no UI changes, safe). T4-T6 add click/lasso/visual feedback. T7-T9 handle multi-drag. T10-T12 are backend (can parallel with T7-T9). T13-T14 wire everything together. T15 validates.\n\nThe SelectedMediaClip type currently has { clipId, assetKey, url, mediaType }. Gallery items won't have clipId or assetKey \u2014 the executor should use empty strings or generate synthetic IDs. The generationId field addition is the key discriminator.\n\nFor the normalizeSelectedClips edge function: it currently filters out items where clip_id is empty.     "PROVIDER-TREE: GallerySelectionProvider must be in AppProviders.tsx (not VideoEditorShell) because GenerationsPane is layout-level while AgentChat is inside VideoEditorShell. Verify both can consume the context.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] vlm-cold-gpu-diagnostics: the cold-gpu vlm import crash investigation is deferred — the plan ships the zombie fix without root-causing the specific import failure. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] workspace-git-hygiene: step 2 discards unstaged edits in 6 files via git checkout head without first confirming the revert was unintentional. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

(2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

Key execution risks: (1) The GallerySelectionProvider must mount in AppProviders.tsx high enough for both GenerationsPane (layout-level) and AgentChat (VideoEditorShell-level) to consume it. (2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline. Gallery items won't have clipId or assetKey \u2014 the executor should use empty strings or generate synthetic IDs. The generationId field addition is the key discriminator.\n\nFor the normalizeSelectedClips edge function: it currently filters out items where clip_id is empty.   "watch_items": [
    "PROVIDER-TREE: GallerySelectionProvider must be in AppProviders.tsx (not VideoEditorShell) because GenerationsPane is layout-level while AgentChat is inside VideoEditorShell. Verify both can consume the context.",
    "NORMALIZE-VALIDATOR: normalizeSelectedClips in index.ts currently requires non-empty clip_id \u2014 gallery items have generation_id but no meaningful clip_id. The validator must be relaxed or gallery items will be silently dropped.",
    "SHARED-COMPONENT: MediaGalleryItem.tsx is used across the entire app (not just editor gallery). New props (isSelected, selectedItems) must be optional and the component must render identically when they're absent.",
    "LASSO-VS-DRAG: Lasso mousedown must only trigger on gallery background/gap, not on items. If it triggers on items, it will conflict with drag-to-timeline. Check event.target carefully.",
    "MULTI-DRAG-ORDER: getMultiGenerationDropData must be checked BEFORE getGenerationDropData in useExternalDrop, otherwise multi-drags will be parsed as single-item drags (first item only).",
    "DEDUP-BY-URL: When merging timeline + gallery clips in AgentChat, deduplicate by URL to avoid sending the same image twice if it appears in both selections.",
    "BACKWARD-COMPAT: Timeline clips must continue working unchanged \u2014 they won't have generationId field. The backend fallback chain is: config.clips lookup \u2192 generation_id field \u2192 skip. Verify the existing path still works.",
    "DEBT-WATCH: agent-chat-rendering debt item \u2014 ensure gallery attachments render correctly in both user and assistant turns. Don't introduce new rendering assumptions.",
    "DEBT-WATCH: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 gallery selections clear after send, but the undo stack won't capture them. This is acceptable (same as timeline selection behavior).",
    "LIGHTBOX-PRESERVATION: With enableSingleClick:true, verify the expand/eye button and double-click path to lightbox still work. The plan assumes these are independent of single-click.",
    "EDGE-FUNCTION-DEPLOY: After T10-T11 changes, the edge function must be deployed before the frontend changes go live: npx supabase functions deploy ai-timeline-agent --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc"

- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

(2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline. - [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

  "watch_items": [
    "PROVIDER-TREE: GallerySelectionProvider must be in AppProviders.tsx (not VideoEditorShell) because GenerationsPane is layout-level while AgentChat is inside VideoEditorShell. Verify both can consume the context.",
    "NORMALIZE-VALIDATOR: normalizeSelectedClips in index.ts currently requires non-empty clip_id \u2014 gallery items have generation_id but no meaningful clip_id. The validator must be relaxed or gallery items will be silently dropped.",
    "SHARED-COMPONENT: MediaGalleryItem.tsx is used across the entire app (not just editor gallery). New props (isSelected, selectedItems) must be optional and the component must render identically when they're absent.",
    "LASSO-VS-DRAG: Lasso mousedown must only trigger on gallery background/gap, not on items. If it triggers on items, it will conflict with drag-to-timeline. Check event.target carefully.",
    "MULTI-DRAG-ORDER: getMultiGenerationDropData must be checked BEFORE getGenerationDropData in useExternalDrop, otherwise multi-drags will be parsed as single-item drags (first item only).",
    "DEDUP-BY-URL: When merging timeline + gallery clips in AgentChat, deduplicate by URL to avoid sending the same image twice if it appears in both selections.",
    "BACKWARD-COMPAT: Timeline clips must continue working unchanged \u2014 they won't have generationId field. The backend fallback chain is: config.clips lookup \u2192 generation_id field \u2192 skip. Verify the existing path still works.",
    "DEBT-WATCH: agent-chat-rendering debt item \u2014 ensure gallery attachments render correctly in both user and assistant turns. Don't introduce new rendering assumptions.",
    "DEBT-WATCH: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 gallery selections clear after send, but the undo stack won't capture them. This is acceptable (same as timeline selection behavior).",
    "LIGHTBOX-PRESERVATION: With enableSingleClick:true, verify the expand/eye button and double-click path to lightbox still work. The plan assumes these are independent of single-click.",
    "EDGE-FUNCTION-DEPLOY: After T10-T11 changes, the edge function must be deployed before the frontend changes go live: npx supabase functions deploy ai-timeline-agent --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc"
  ],

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] workspace-git-hygiene: step 2 discards unstaged edits in 6 files via git checkout head without first confirming the revert was unintentional. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Batch 6 is in progress. I’m tracing the drag path from `MediaGalleryItem` into the shared drag helpers now, because this batch hinges on preserving the single-item drag behavior while only switching to the multi-item payload when the dragged item is part of a real multi-selection.

> AGENT

I’ve settled on the smallest safe path: keep selection data flowing through props only, derive the multi-drag set in the grid, and let the item switch between single and multi drag payloads based on `isSelected` plus the selected-items count. The next patch adds that and covers both branches in the behavior test.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Gallery media selection for timeline agent context. Extend the media selection pattern used on the timeline to also work in the generation gallery pane and gallery view. Users should be able to: 1) Single-click to select one item, 2) Cmd+click to select multiple items individually, 3) Selected items should be visually highlighted similar to timeline selection, 4) Selected gallery items should appear in the agent chat panel as context (same 'attaching N images' indicator), 5) Selected gallery items should be sent alongside tasks to the timeline agent, same as timeline selections. Key design goal: generalize the selection system so it works for both timeline clips and gallery items. The selection hook/context should be shared or at least use a common interface. Gallery selection applies to images and videos only (not effects, not music). The existing useSelectedMediaClips hook returns clip-based data — we need to either extend it or create a parallel hook for gallery items that produces the same output shape. The agent chat and task submission already accept attachments — we need to feed gallery selections into the same pipeline.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Additive — both timeline and gallery selections contribute to attachments. Q2: Double-click still opens lightbox. Single click selects. Q3: Only in GenerationsPane within editor for now. Q4: Shift+click to toggle individual items one at a time (not range select). Also support lasso/marquee drag-to-select multiple items. Additionally: selected gallery items (whether selected via lasso, shift+click, or single click) should be draggable onto the video editor timeline. When multiple items are selected, dragging them should drag all selected items together onto the timeline.

Key execution risks: (1) The GallerySelectionProvider must mount in AppProviders.tsx high enough for both GenerationsPane (layout-level) and AgentChat (VideoEditorShell-level) to consume it. (2) The normalizeSelectedClips function in the edge function currently requires clip_id to be non-empty \u2014 gallery items won't have meaningful clip_ids, so the validator must be relaxed to accept items with generation_id instead. (3) MediaGalleryItem is a shared component used across the app \u2014 changes to props/behavior must not break non-editor gallery views. (4) The lasso hook needs careful mousedown target detection to avoid conflicting with item drag-to-timeline. (5) Multi-item drag requires a new drag type that the timeline drop handler must explicitly check before the existing single-item path.\n\nExecution order: T1-T3 are foundation (no UI changes, safe). Gallery items won't have clipId or assetKey \u2014 the executor should use empty strings or generate synthetic IDs.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] drop-indicator-plumbing: dropindicator reads position.trackid without it being declared on the dropindicatorposition interface or forwarded by toindicatorposition(). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: legacy persisted 'enhance' values might exist in storage (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no-migration assumption for enhance→upscale is under-supported (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: enhance/upscale only partially resolved — persisted values not validated (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
